# Validation, Benchmark & Migration Specification

**狀態：未執行的驗收規格。** Source `71cc55d`／release1.3.2；目標1.4.0。不能把本表中的expected結果當成observed結果。真正實作時保存每次command、SHA、artifactchecksum與環境，不提交runtime配置/credentials/logs/worlds/report。

## V00 — 建置與套件gate

1.4.0驗收前先完成T00A：Gradle9.8.0、Java25 launcher/daemon/toolchain、`options.release=25`、official distribution checksum與所有classfile tooling相容。用`./gradlew.bat --version`／`javaToolchains`核對，JAR目標classfile major69；production/API/PAPI/acceptance一致。1.3.2原建置另留Java21／Gradle9.1.0，勿修改舊artifact充當baseline。
Root既有commands（T14後須保留aliases）：

```powershell
.\gradlew.bat test :syncmoney-papi-expansion:test shadowJar :syncmoney-papi-expansion:jar acceptanceJar
pnpm --dir syncmoney-web typecheck
pnpm --dir syncmoney-web test:unit --run
pnpm --dir syncmoney-web build
```

Frontend改动后另跑 `pnpm --dir syncmoney-web build:embedded`；此command会改generateddist与WebAdminServerfilelists，二者一并review。只进行本次文件计划验证不需要执行此command。

未来新task（不是现有能力）：

```powershell
.\gradlew.bat verifyArchitecture verifyPackaging
.\gradlew.bat :syncmoney-core:test :syncmoney-protocol:test :syncmoney-api:check
.\gradlew.bat :syncmoney-storage:integrationTest :syncmoney-sync:integrationTest
```

`verifyArchitecture`检查Gradle resolved compile/runtime deps与bytecode：API→JDK only；core→api/JDK；protocol→JDK+明确JSON；无forbiddenrefs或cycles。`verifyPackaging`检查actualshadowJAR：mainclass/descriptor/Lua/YAML/embeddedassets齐全、API不relocate且onlyoneclass、JDBCServiceLoader与SQLitenative资源可用、Adventure/Vault/PAPI/CMIserverruntime未shade、没有testkit/JMH/probeclasses。Rootoutputpaths维持原样。

## V01 — correctness/property suite

固定合成账户 A `00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001`、B `...0002`，BigDecimal两位，round `1.005→1.01`。余额初始化由fixtureload完成，不在timed/critical section强行createaccount。

| case | assertion | task |
|---|---|---|
| rejectednegative/zero/out-of-range | 无balance/version/queuedwork变化 | T01/T02 |
| withdrawal concurrentinsufficient | 正确该次结果，不从其他writer版本猜成功 | T02 |
| receivercancels transfer100/0→10 | 最终100/0，总量100，无单腿遗留 | T07 |
| fulladmission + alloverflowunavailable | 立即拒绝，无mutation或成功event | T03 |
| coldexistingbalance100 | no syncI/O；miss不persist0；asyncready后100 | T04 |
| multipleasync loads sameUUID | 一个loader，pendingmutation不被stalesnapshot盖掉 | T04 |
| confirmedRedis90/v7 then concurrent v8 | 同receipt90/7进wire和SQL，不配90/8 | T05 |
| SQL v41 followed delayedv40 | row不regress（仅新writer）；同v不同moneyconflict | T05 |
| Lua wrongreceiver/versiontype/overflow | 第一次mutation前reject；余额不变 | T06 |
| alreadyexecuted,response lost | 去重确认或quarantine，不能第二次ADD | T06 |
| markerabsent + SENT_UNKNOWN | 无blind retry，即使未满TTL | T06/T08 |
| two-nodecached100 eachwithdraw80 | 明确authorityreject、pendingrebuild/unavailable，不能假globalfinal | T07 |
| partialpairSQL | transactionrollback/整对stale拒绝；不能onlyoneleg持久化 | T07 |
| recoveryread/crash/ack/compact | 原记录不先clear，成功后checkpoint | T08 |
| pendingwriteeviction/logout | 不丢事件或confirmedbase/pendingowner | T03/T15 |
| pluginAPIattribution/hooks | 正确name，一次pre/post/completion；cancel无资金变化 | T02/T11 |
| restartrequired reload | snapshot/owner均不halfapply | T09 |
| schemaDDLfailed | metadata不advance；newerschema不隐式变更 | T09 |
| equal/older remote | noappliedupdate/nooverwrite；unknownenum可读 | T10 |
| CMIclock/retirement | freshness在ownercallback再次判定、noecho | T13 |
| shutdownqueueemptyretryfull | drain/recordpending、storage仍live | T15 |

所有concurrencyfixture采用latches/barriers/manualexecutor，禁止靠sleep通过率证明正确。随机propertytests固定seed并记录失败seed；fakecontract与realbackends相同。

## V02 — protocol／数据fixtures

保存actual1.3.2 codec/source读取后的合成goldens：optionalomitted、primitiveamount0.0、unknownevent/field、UUIDbad、missingfield、非finite金额、long>2^53、CMIeventtype、sourceServer/messageIdecho、reorder/duplicate。Java读long不得經JS浮點roundtrip。

FreezeLua KEY/ARGV/return/errorcontract，特别set键顺序是version/balance，add是balance/version。原数值double与截断结果不能被提取顺手改成decimalstring。脚本修复另保存before/afterexpectation。

SQL fixtures必须分别：

- shared PostgreSQL/MySQL实际 `players.player_uuid`、DECIMAL20,2、BIGINT与schema table。
- MariaDB通过既有mysqlconnection配置真实启动，不能假設有独立正式mode。
- LOCAL SQLite `player_balances`/`transactions` 的REAL/int既有格式与同路径。
- audit id='audit'/11、main id='1'/1、baltop、Shadow；disabledmodule不创建它的schema。
- configschema12、messages/alias、unknownoperator键（值都是无secret合成值）。

## V03 — 正式runtime／upgrade matrix

**必測minimum：Paper1.21.6＋Java25＋Syncmoney1.4.0。** Java25是最低runtime非僅build JDK，所有1.4classes target25；Paper API/descriptor minimum是1.21.6。以下記exact build/hash、JVM/dependencies/artifacts，compile成功不是runtime通過。

| contract | platform／runtime | scenario與gate |
|---|---|---|
| Required minimum | Paper1.21.6 pinned build / Java25 / 1.4 | actual load、Vault與VaultUnlocked分別、read SDK discovery/classloader、cold/warm/offline、LOCAL/SYNC/LOCAL_REDIS適用入口、enabled/disabled、shutdown/drain |
| Required current supported Paper | 執行時最新正式supported Paper，固定取得日期/build / Java25 / 1.4 | 同integration/failure suite；不單寫latest、不以1.21.8當minimum |
| Required state/config upgrade | 1.3.2 persisted fixtures → 1.4，目標至少Paper1.21.6及最新supported Paper / Java25 | 不reset balance/config/SQL/Redis；M00–M12，原地升級/rollback保留新交易 |
| Supported Folia | 依現有Folia support contract取可用且project支持的exact build / Java25 | 兩region、player owner、CMI callback retirement、teleport/logout/lifecycle；既有26.2 beta是歷史observed baseline，非唯一永久build要求 |
| Supported Canvas | 依現有Canvas contract驗證exact build / Java25 | owner/lifecycle/guard combinations；無證據不可列PASS |
| Two-backend network | 兩個supported Paper pinned builds、既有proxy契約 / Java25 | distinctserver-name、isolatedRedis/PostgreSQL、跨服pay/reconnect/retry/drain |

### T21 execution checkpoint — 2026-10-12 (IN_PROGRESS)

#### M01–M03 bounded SYNC minimum-Paper subcase — 2026-10-13

**Result: PASS_BOUNDED_SUBCASE, E2.** Machine report: `build/reports/foundation/T21-sync-upgrade/paper-1.21.6-48-20261013-003948-b2643f6e128e4ad2afd0cbf867e77ffa/result.json`, SHA-256 `95da356faf7883abe3ca104496f41b997e0c0cf024f453911cc2fdd71725b090`. It exercised one Paper `1.21.6#48` node / Java `25.0.4+7-LTS-189` with isolated PostgreSQL `18.1` and Redis `8.10.1` (AOF enabled, `appendfsync always`, database 15), baseline Syncmoney `1.3.2` SHA-256 `442a404276b538ce807b0f063fb37920be85e60961a52a475b3f3524d08f4f10` at source commit `71cc55dfeefe9a8f40a3835b051ddd324d353767`, and candidate `1.4.0-alpha.1` SHA-256 `55d42c2b72e86fdfd1d36e4f909e140f88256527a8e189c0c699d0cef89935d0`. Paper JAR SHA-256 was `35e2dfa66b3491b9d2f0bb033679fa5aca1e1fdf097e7a06a80ce8afeda5c214`; local PlugDev CLI SHA-256 was `3a600872a3c95bb67b960bdb5efcbb84eb675110f89146566bedb1835ad047d7`.

The 1.3.2 baseline seeded `AcceptanceA=1000.00/v1` and `AcceptanceB=520.00/v1` through the registered Vault provider. On 1.4, `smaccept write` deposited `337.25` and `245.50` using Vault `depositPlayer`; both `EconomyResponse`s reported `SUCCESS`, the exact amount and resulting balance, and the in-memory state matched. The candidate API consumer logged `API CLASSLOADER PASS`. After normal candidate shutdown, SQL and Redis both contained `1337.25/v2` and `765.50/v2`. The 1.3.2 rollback read those same values and normal shutdown retained version 2; rollback did not reset Redis or restore the pre-upgrade dump. All three server stop commands exited 0 and each run-marked Paper process exited.

Config assertions ran at baseline seeding, candidate start/stop, and rollback start/stop (five observations). Every observation retained config schema 12, SYNC mode, the enabled isolated PostgreSQL database `syncmoney_t21`, Redis database 15, legacy database/Pub/Sub switches, and the synthetic operator fixture. The eight pre-upgrade PostgreSQL/config/Redis backup entries matched their reported hashes. The candidate's existing recovery journal was present, six frames (four RECORD, two ACK), SHA-256 `e42f96b475e53f7b127ed03ab0c4c217fd2a20cc016ddf99b63a6641a7272c71`, independently revalidated as `PASS_FORMAT_AND_CHECKSUM`, with zero pending operations before rollback.

The report pins the runner (`1f4e8d49003e2d21d098febfda171f1ecf53b35b338501357020f8a08e0d2ca6`), recovery validator (`a429c3638af0da00697138108da3f96513fe463bd23faabe63b93b74efdc1107`), cleanup helper (`541ab24db8646334301131cdcae1d13a719dce440da5a230162ff896e8f5aec3`), acceptance artifact (`d1e71f3a00cc2ffb075b968ae442e589deb1c2c5b8e511b75321b659b5f52ed2`), API consumer (`b35f098f969387153f1feca27f1ea2535d522a859a69974a5d9d6960ab50169d`), and stage-specific runtime plugin JAR hashes. Post-run checks independently rehashed all eight backups and the recovery journal, confirmed candidate hash equality, and found no listener on the run's game/RCON/PostgreSQL/Redis ports and no run-marked JVM.

**Validation:** Java `25.0.4` / Gradle `9.8.0`: `test :syncmoney-papi-expansion:test shadowJar :syncmoney-papi-expansion:jar acceptanceJar apiConsumerJar --rerun-tasks` → BUILD SUCCESSFUL, 53/53 tasks; 141 JUnit XML files contained 680 tests, zero failures/errors/skips. Log SHA-256 `a1a23fada4558033f4ae997196728740627c361311e53bae34389dbf34cc959d`. `node --test tools/plugdev/prepare.test.mjs` → 8/8; `pwsh -NoProfile -File tools/plugdev/recovery-spool-validator.test.ps1` → 9 cases PASS; `pwsh -NoProfile -File tools/plugdev/run-sync-upgrade-cleanup.test.ps1` → all 4 scenarios PASS; PowerShell parser → 5/5 scripts; `git diff --check` → PASS. Java 21 negative-load check was run separately against the same candidate hash: `javap` observed class-file major 69 and JDK 21 rejected `noietime.syncmoney.Syncmoney` with `UnsupportedClassVersionError` (JDK 21 maximum major 65).

**Failed attempts and disposition:** the first instrumented run exposed a harness scalar `.Count` assumption on an empty log-marker result; changed the marker collection to an explicit array and length. The next run intentionally used the old compiled acceptance probe and failed at `baseline-write` because it emitted only `T21_WRITE_PASS a=... b=...` after direct facade writes and provided no Vault receipt; it exited cleanly and is not product-failure evidence. After rebuilding the probe with Vault mutations and response assertions, the final bounded cycle passed. These failed reports remain in ignored `build/reports/foundation/T21-sync-upgrade/` and do not contribute to PASS.

**M01–M03 runner cleanup regression rerun — PASS_BOUNDED_SUBCASE (2026-10-13).** The accepted rerun is `build/reports/foundation/T21-sync-upgrade/paper-1.21.6-48-20261013-011018-cdad62b820b943f98ed58a04d1dd6c00/result.json` (SHA-256 `89f93dcd7f8f5a0cdcc30a61efc9cfe26890c83130fadf60703a798959311aab`). It repeated the same minimum-Paper single-node SYNC baseline → candidate Vault writes/normal drain → 1.3.2 rollback sequence and specifically verified the new isolated Gradle-daemon cleanup: owned PID `20696` was found using this run's isolated Gradle User Home, the run wrapper's `--stop` exited 0, and no matching daemon remained. Each of the three Paper normal stops exited 0; the report's `failure` and `cleanupFailure` are null. All eight backup artifacts matched their hashes, and the existing six-frame candidate `recovery-v1.jsonl` passed format/checksum validation with zero pending records. Focused validation: cleanup-helper tests passed all 9 cases; PlugDev preparation tests passed 8/8 from the repository root; recovery-spool validator tests passed 9/9; five PowerShell scripts parsed; `git diff --check` passed. This remains one-node bounded evidence and does not prove fleet quiescence, queue/retry/in-flight counters for a production fleet, or the remaining runtime matrix.

**Limit:** this is not M01–M03 aggregate acceptance and does not establish fleet-wide producer quiescence, full queue/retry/in-flight drain counters, every mode/backend/platform/failure combination, rollback of SQLite/fleet config, hard-crash or power-loss recovery, Folia/Canvas scheduler behavior, or licensed CMI runtime. T21 stays IN_PROGRESS; licensed CMI remains BLOCKED / NOT RUN for lack of an authorized runtime JAR. User-confirmed platform endpoints remain validation targets, not blanket support claims.

Each report contains six startup/runtime Syncmoney pins across baseline, candidate, and rollback stages on both node identities; the candidate Vault API consumer and write receipts passed. SQL and Redis snapshots agree at every observed stage: `1000.00/v1, 520.00/v1` before upgrade; `1337.25/v2, 765.50/v2` after the first candidate node; and `1444.25/v3, 820.50/v3` after the second candidate node and both 1.3.2 rollback reads. Each report has six healthy normal server stops and two six-frame candidate recovery journals; both journals were byte-identical at rollback, passed format/checksum validation, and had zero pending records. Each report also records all four verified backend stops at exactly `preupgrade-backup` and `final-cleanup`: Redis `SHUTDOWN SAVE` exit 0 with process exit 0; PostgreSQL running status exit 0 with PID marker, `pg_ctl stop` exit 0, and exact stopped status exit 3. Isolated Gradle `--stop` exited 0 with no matching daemon remaining.

The cleanup gate now fails closed if a ready Redis process exits before the requested `SHUTDOWN SAVE`; it only records success after command and process exit are verified. PostgreSQL running status requires exit 0 and a PID marker; stopped status requires exit 3 and the exact `pg_ctl: no server running` text. Unknown PostgreSQL status triggers a bounded stop against the isolated data directory, but the run remains failed even if the subsequent stop is verified. A PASS additionally requires one successful Redis and PostgreSQL observation at each of the two required stages, both started flags cleared, and no `cleanupFailure`. The runner also gates exact stop-stage coverage per node and the deployed Syncmoney JAR SHA-256 on every baseline/candidate/rollback start. The 16-case cleanup test and 29-case T21 validation test include negative cases for the rejected states and mismatched pins.

**Verification:** `pwsh -NoProfile -File build/reports/foundation/T21-sync-upgrade/verify-corrected-platform-sync-reports.ps1` exited 0 and reported all seven retained reports final=True for runtime identity, lifecycle, plugin pins, backend shutdown coverage, SQL/Redis parity, recovery journals, Gradle cleanup, and clean exit. Focused checks: cleanup helper 16/16; T21 validation 29/29; recovery-spool validator 12/12; PlugDev preparation 8/8; PowerShell parser 10/10; Java 25.0.4 / Gradle 9.8.0 `:acceptanceJar --rerun-tasks` BUILD SUCCESSFUL (13 tasks); `git diff --check` PASS. The independent current-runner source review found no actionable findings. At this checkpoint the independent evidence review was pending. A later independent audit/review covers the six user-pinned endpoint reports only; it does not cover historical Paper `26.3#175`, which remains NOT independently reviewed and excluded from acceptance. The supplemental current-stable Paper `26.3#178` report is recorded separately below.

**Limits / disposition:** These are sequential, bounded two-node SYNC cases, not fleet-wide producer fencing or simultaneous mixed-version writes. No full queue/retry/in-flight/audit counters are exposed by this runtime; those values remain NOT EXPOSED / NOT TESTED. The reports do not establish hard-crash/power-loss recovery, all mode/backend/dialect/failure combinations, Folia/Canvas entity-scheduler safety, or licensed CMI behavior. The broader V03 default quiesced network-upgrade gate remains open and T21 remains IN_PROGRESS. **Runner discovery and retry disposition:** Redis `8.10.1` omits `aof_last_load_status` from `INFO persistence`. The harness therefore requires AOF enabled, rejects a present status other than `ok`, and always verifies exact balance/version readback after restart; absence of this field is not treated as a Redis durability claim. Six earlier Paper `1.21.6#48` attempts failed before the accepted final run due to harness setup/observation issues: transient listener ownership lookup, command result shape, candidate artifact resolution, a success marker absent during polling but present in the saved server log, reading an incremental AOF while Redis still held it open, and assuming `aof_last_load_status` exists. The file-hash check now occurs after verified graceful Redis shutdown. Final harness checks passed PowerShell parsing, AOF status predicate 5/5, and `node --test tools/plugdev/prepare.test.mjs` 7/7; two final minimum-Paper runs passed. Independent shutdown/lifecycle review and targeted AOF-status review found no actionable remaining issue. Normal shutdown left an internal `recovery-v1.jsonl` spool (3,409 bytes; four RECORD and two ACK frames); independent frame folding found zero pending records. This is an observation of the normal-drain path, not proof of hard-crash durability, power-loss recovery, or downgrade safety. M06 does not establish scheduler behavior for Folia/Canvas or complete V03 compatibility.

**Harness discovery and disposition:** The first PostgreSQL attempt did not wait for a new asynchronous acceptance completion marker before issuing stop. Its eventual server log contains `ACCEPTANCE PASS`, but the post-stop SQL snapshot has only AcceptanceA's baltop row; it is excluded as an invalid harness attempt, not product-failure evidence. The invalid session log is `postgres-paper-invalid-first-session.log.gz` (SHA-256 `89bbbe66df3c7699d4867d37e52c5a01a0d7d9ee25ab9bdaaf6f48e56256ec70`), the stop-timeline log is `postgres-paper-first-attempt-invalid-shutdown.log` (SHA-256 `2025f3beee303ceaa4fc7432c63ad14d7a289fde94c293a6bc03f3cd02bd44fa`), and its one-row SQL snapshot is `postgres-poststop-sql.txt` (SHA-256 `9ef49846d7994ee97fe486adb99b14f4dd7dba1f82ae2ab901684f23d42568f2`). The corrected retry used a completion-marker barrier and normal stop; its accepted SQL snapshot contains both accounts and Redis parity. `tools/plugdev/README.md` now captures the pre-command log boundary and waits for fresh success markers before stopping; the RCON acknowledgement itself is not treated as completion.

## V04 — Vault与PAPI兼容

保存1.3.2compiledconsumer（非只重新compile）测试旧getter/descriptors；新consumercompileOnlyAPI并真实ServicesManagerlookup。測read cached100、offline100未warm、confirmed absent empty而無帳戶建立、unavailable exceptional、providerstop後read引用；notreadywithdraw只測既有Vault/internal，非new SDK方法。

明确coldqueryfallback与coldmutation失败behavior是1.4设计变化，不能仅warmbench后说Vault全部seamless。真实第三方至少选择一个常用shop/reward integration与offlinequery consumer，记录版本和功能；不将mockprovider当生态覆盖。

PAPI：oldexpansion/newplugin、newexpansion/oldplugin、新新；全部families、`balance()` accessorfallback、5秒/10000bounds、noI/Oplaceholder。不新增未成熟rank PublicAPI来强迫改PAPI。

## V05 — CMI与可选模块

LicensedCMI必须维护者本机提供并记录版本，不下载未授权jar。Unitreflectionfake只能证明adapterlogic，不能写成realCMIpass。Clockskew/backward/restart/counterwrap由deterministicclock；scheduledcallbackdelayed/retired/remoteecho由realowner验证。CMI和旧wiretimestampordering限制明写。

组合至少：globalbreaker off/playerprotection on；audit off/cleanup-export开配置；web off；shadow off；LOCAL Redis/SQLoff；LOCAL_REDIS SQLoff；任一initstagefail与closefail。Assert不存在该module的heavyresources，guard唯一owner。

## B00 — Benchmark profiles与command合同

As of 2026-10-09, T19 implements these Gradle tasks and runner CLI in `:syncmoney-benchmarks`; use the commands below only when the required hash-bound baseline pilot and endpoint configuration are available.

loadTest requires a hash-bound 1.3.2 Redis/SQL macro-pilot manifest; the synthetic facade acceptance rate from runBaselineComparison is explicitly invalid for load calibration. The baseline JAR is rebuilt with Java 21, while both macro pilot and current measurement must run on Java 25. Keep Redis/SQL endpoint credentials in the named environment variables and out of commands/reports.

```powershell
.\gradlew.bat :syncmoney-benchmarks:benchmarkJar
java -jar syncmoney-benchmarks/build/libs/SyncmoneyBenchmarks.jar -wi 5 -i 10 -w 1s -r 1s -f 3 -prof gc -jvmArgsAppend '-XX:+UseSerialGC -Dnet.bytebuddy.experimental=true -Xmx1g' -rf json -rff build/reports/benchmarks/jmh-1.4.0.json
.\gradlew.bat :syncmoney-benchmarks:runBaselineComparison '-PbaselineCheckout=C:/absolute/disposable/verified-1.3.2'
.\gradlew.bat :syncmoney-benchmarks:loadTest -Pprofile=syncmoney-benchmarks/profiles/redis-sql-open-loop.json '-PbaselineCheckout=C:/absolute/disposable/verified-1.3.2' '-PbaselinePilot=C:/absolute/evidence/verified-1.3.2-redis-sql-macro-pilot.json'
```

JMH runner使用明确annotationprocessor/version锁定，独立fork，不在IDE跑。JMHparams：accountcount1/1000/10000，producer1/2/8，queuecapacitylegacy50000与小capacity压力case，hotkey集中与均匀。通过@Setup预热/初始化/重置，各runpostcondition检查资金与版本，不在measuredoperation准备环境。

| benchmark | variant | 输出 |
|---|---|---|
| balance read | warmed、cold/unready异步schedule separate | ns/op、bytes/op、cachedstatus |
| deposit/withdraw/set | success、reject、cancel、hotcontention | ops/s、sampletime、alloc、outcomerate |
| transfer | localpair、Redisconfirmedasync分开 | acceptance/receiptlatency、conservation |
| queue | reserve/commit/dequeue、full、多producer | offered/accepted/reject、tail、CPU、GC |
| serialization | legacyminimal/full/CMI/badJSON | bytes/op、encode/decode latency、wireequivalence |
| messageprocess | newer/equal/older/duplicate/burst | apply/rejectrate、alloc、lag |
| batchpersistence | batch1/50/200/500 + maxwait1/5/20ms | rows/s、commitlatency、oldestage、rollback |
| macro pipeline | Redis/SQL healthy/slow/down/reconnect | p50/95/99/99.9、reject、persisted、backlogrepair |

主要paired profile固定JDK25/heap/GC、hardwareCPU/RAM/OS、flags、CPU affinity/power settings、Redis/SQL版本與network；1.3.2使用原Java21編譯artifact在Java25執行，1.4.0使用Java25編譯artifact，兩者測量JVM相同。另跑1.3.2/Java21作歷史profile、1.3.2/Java25作JVM影響profile；不可把Java21舊結果直接與Java25新結果混成架構收益。若舊artifact無法於Java25執行，標paired比較BLOCKED，先處理baseline harness相容且保留舊語義，不虛構數值。交錯跑避免温度/CPU噪聲；3 forks不是p99.9充分樣本保證。Java25.0.4 Windows host上的paired JMH先後在G1與ParallelGC profile出現native process crash；根因未知。同一失敗列各在ParallelGC與SerialGC下各成功跑完一次，不足以判定根因；依ADR-17，paired及current-only JMH改用**僅限benchmark量測的SerialGC profile**，並保留ADR-08全部correctness/sample gates。Macro pilot與open-loop仍維持ParallelGC profile：r2 macro pilot曾通過，open-loop尚未執行；它們是分開的Redis/SQL runner，不作JMH stability的替代證據。這些GC選擇不改Syncmoney runtime GC，也不宣稱HotSpot根因已知。

所有macro-pilot、open-loop及JMH測量fork固定最大heap為1 GiB，並使用ByteBuddy flag。Paired與current-only JMH使用SerialGC；macro pilot與open-loop使用ParallelGC。每次paired comparison的baseline/candidate必須共用相同JDK、GC、heap與JVM flags，不得混算不同profile。Macro pilot保留response/receipt/durable/accepted-time樣本直到報告生成，因此每次measurement最多允許1,000,000個offered樣本；profile要求更高minimum時必須在連接Redis/SQL前拒絕。此上限仍高於100,000個p99.9最低樣本門檻10倍；超限時measurement失敗，不截斷樣本或輸出部分valid報告。

Macroopenloop到达率用baseline可持续acceptedrate的25/50/75/100/125%，不是手写漂亮ops数；每档120秒warm+300秒measurement起始、≥100000samples，实际profile由pilot确认。记录**scheduledarrival到response**（含schedlag），另记录accept到confirmedreceipt/durablecommit；reject也是response样本。HDR原始/expectedintervalcorrected分开标，closedloop用于diagnostic不能替代openloop。

Result JSON fields：runId、sourceSHA、baselineSHA、artifactSHA256、profileId、JDK/OS/hardware/deps、flags、dataset、duration/sampleCount、offered/accepted/rejected/persisted、responseHistogram、receiptHistogram、durableHistogram、allocationBytesPerOp、GC、queueMax/oldestAge、ambiguous/quarantine、moneyInvariantFailures、versionConflicts、notes。含counts不足时标 p99.9不可靠，不能造confidenceinterval。

**Performance regression/review gates（非hard money gate）：** matched p99≤baseline110%、allocation/op≤110%、throughput≥90%；p99.9結合confidence/samplecount。超過須profile/cost分析與maintainer documented exception，correctness修復的合理成本可接受；不靜默改threshold／刪rejected samples。**Hard blockers：** money invariant violation、confirmed loss/corruption、contract禁止的incorrect success、unsafe unbounded pipeline、禁止hotpath network/SQL/filesystem blocking。這些即使性能很好仍FAIL，不能performance exception。Cold behavior差異不得冒充收益。

**Agrona合并条件：** 同contract/全部faultsuite、无unboundedspin/更高idleCPU，allocation或tail有可重复收益且maintainerreview复杂度；不满足保留JDKqueue。Batchcoalescing只能safeabsoluteSQLsnapshot，不能丢audit或ADDrequest identity、不能穿SET/transferbarrier。

### T19 JMH dependency research

Checked 2026-10-09 against first-party JMH/OpenJDK metadata and official Gradle documentation:

- **Version:** JMH `1.37` is the newest version tag observed in the [official JMH tags](https://github.com/openjdk/jmh/tags) (tag commit `2effa2c8310e1d3ad03c8ee02024edca9252b46a`). Maven Central metadata independently reports `latest` and `release` as `1.37` for both [`jmh-core`](https://repo.maven.apache.org/maven2/org/openjdk/jmh/jmh-core/maven-metadata.xml) and [`jmh-generator-annprocess`](https://repo.maven.apache.org/maven2/org/openjdk/jmh/jmh-generator-annprocess/maven-metadata.xml).
- **Version alignment / processor:** The [JMH 1.37 benchmark archetype POM](https://github.com/openjdk/jmh/blob/1.37/jmh-archetypes/jmh-java-benchmark-archetype/src/main/resources/archetype-resources/pom.xml) pins both artifacts to the same `${jmh.version}`; the tagged [annotation-processor module POM](https://github.com/openjdk/jmh/blob/1.37/jmh-generator-annprocess/pom.xml) inherits parent version `1.37` and depends on `jmh-core` at `${project.version}`. JMH's [README](https://github.com/openjdk/jmh/blob/1.37/README.md) says annotation/bytecode processing is needed to generate benchmark code and that `jmh-core` alone is insufficient; it recommends a standalone benchmark project and describes a separate subproject as customary for large projects.
- **Narrow dependency arrangement:** use the existing `:syncmoney-benchmarks` subproject without a JMH Gradle plugin; define one fixed `jmhVersion = "1.37"`, put `org.openjdk.jmh:jmh-core:$jmhVersion` on that project's implementation/runtime path, and explicitly put `org.openjdk.jmh:jmh-generator-annprocess:$jmhVersion` on its `annotationProcessor` path. Keep both out of every production module and production artifact; only the isolated benchmark runner may package JMH runtime dependencies.
- **Java 25 compatibility:** first-party JMH 1.37 CI still does not claim Java 25 support, but the T19 local compile/build and Java 25 runner smoke now pass. The exact observed scope and remaining full-measurement limitations are recorded under Executed evidence — T19 below.

HDR Histogram dependency research checked 2026-10-09 against first-party HdrHistogram tags and Maven Central metadata: `org.hdrhistogram:HdrHistogram:2.2.2` is the latest observed release. Its tagged POM targets Java 8 bytecode. Its official CI stops at Java 21, so Java 25 compatibility is UNKNOWN until the isolated T19 runner compiles and exercises the histogram dependency. Keep it benchmark-only; no production module or plugin artifact may depend on it. Sources: https://github.com/HdrHistogram/HdrHistogram/tags, https://repo.maven.apache.org/maven2/org/hdrhistogram/HdrHistogram/maven-metadata.xml, https://github.com/HdrHistogram/HdrHistogram/blob/HdrHistogram-2.2.2/pom.xml, https://github.com/HdrHistogram/HdrHistogram/blob/HdrHistogram-2.2.2/.github/workflows/maven.yml.

## M00 — Upgrade engineering conditions

Supported upgrade：Existing Syncmoney1.3.2 persisted balance/config/SQL/Redis可原地升1.4而不reset，前提Paper≥1.21.6、Java≥25與健康配置/依賴。Paper≤1.21.5或Java≤24先升platform/runtime，不屬直接runtime guarantee；無需保留舊platform support。另確認唯一server-name、mode、backup/drain、無ambiguous recovery；不自動修壞moneykey或換authority。

**2026-10-05 T02 outage behavior / migration note:** in shared/synchronized modes, a known Redis-degraded state rejects new deposit, withdraw, and SET requests before memory mutation, transaction events, or queue admission. LOCAL remains on its SQLite path. Work accepted before the outage stays in the existing in-process write/retry tracking and resumes against Redis after recovery; SQL is not promoted to shared authority. This narrows the 1.3.2 degraded DB-fallback behavior. No config, schema, or Redis wire change is made; `pay.allow-in-degraded` retains its existing transfer-only meaning and default. This is E1 fixture evidence only: pending work is not hard-crash durable, and shutdown/restart recovery remains outside T02 (E08 stays INCONCLUSIVE/P0-CANDIDATE and non-mandatory).

### M01 — 备份与维护窗口

- [ ] 先確認Java≥25、Paper≥1.21.6 actual service/container啟動與dependencies；舊JVM/server先升环境，保留完整回復配置，不reset SQL/Redis/balances。原1.3.2 JAR在新環境若不支援，採離線合成fixture資料遷入supported環境驗證，不要求1.4維護舊platform。
- [ ] 记录source/JARsha、Java/server/deps、configschema、SQLmain/auditschema、Redis版本与topology；不输出secret。
- [ ] 全node关闭经济producer／third-party自动reward与管理员写入，确认API已unregister/healthquiescing。
- [ ] drain queue、inflight、retry、DB與audit至zero或稳定可确认pending；pending无法确认则停止upgrade。
- [ ] 正常stop所有nodes，备份config/messages/aliases、SQL、LOCALSQLite及WAL/SHM（优先SQLitebackupAPI或完全关闭后拷贝）、RedisRDB/AOF、overflow/recovery；保存preupgradehash。

### M02 — 更新与canary

- [ ] 每台plugins仅一份 `Syncmoney-1.4.0.jar`，主类/datafolder不变；PAPIexpansion仍可旧版起步验证。
- [ ] 先canary，关闭producer；确认startup/schema/key/config保留，API/Vaultready、optionaldisabled符合，webunsafe例外明确。
- [ ] 使用**disposable acceptance账户**执行read/write验证（productioncanary测试须当时批准小范围写入），正确handler/codec与receipt；不拿真用户余额做破坏性fault。
- [ ] 其余nodes升级，各节点ready且无mixedoldwriter后恢复producer。记录升级前后总量、版本与reconcile差异；健康OK不等于数据验收自动通过。

### M03 — 正常降级

- [ ] 1.4.0 JAR不能在Java21載入。plugin降回1.3.2可先保留已驗證的Java25；若另需降JVM，須配回支援Java21的server與dependencies，26.2新server不可假定能降回Java21。
- [ ] 再quiesce所有nodes并drain，保存**当前最新数据**与現有recovery紀錄（若採newspool也保存）。
- [ ] 若ADR-07核准new `recovery-v1.jsonl`，其pending/ambiguous必须已解决，不能给旧reader忽略后说rollback成功。
- [ ] 换回verified1.3.2JAR，保留当前compatibleSQL/Redis/SQLite/config数据；不清Redis、不把version归零。
- [ ] 读取upgrade期间新交易并验证仍在；旧writer重新引入已知stale写入限制，downgrade说明恢复该限制。
- [ ] 旧backup只用于另行批准的灾难恢复；restore会丢升级后合法交易，不能算seamlessrollback。

没有destructiveDDL或incompatiblewire并不自动证明rollback，newrecovery与behavior也要验收。

## M04–M12 — 数据／mixed-version矩阵

| ID | old→new/new→old scenarios | expected/releasepolicy |
|---|---|---|
| M04 | LOCALsyntheticSQLitewithhistoricaltransactions | schema/path/REAL值可读、normaldrain两方向 |
| M05 | SYNCPostgreSQL/MySQL/MariaDB | player_uuid与schemaids不变、新guardwrites不会回退 |
| M06 | LOCAL_REDIS/AOFrestart | legacykey/result相容，noSQLresource；实际Redisdurability依配置 |
| M07 | CMIold/newwireclockskew | 明确freshness/collisionlimitations，licensedscenario |
| M08 | config12/customkeys/oldschemafailedbackup | preserve/idempotent/fail原档、reload不halfapply |
| M09 | old→new、新→oldJSON/unknownfield/CMIlong | codec互通，duplicate/stale无apply |
| M10 | disconnect/reconnect/duplicate/receivedelayed | at-most-once丢訊後可reconcile，noecho |
| M11 | oldSQLv40在newv41之后 | **预期能展示legacyrace，不能宣称live-mixed强一致** |
| M12 | old/newnodes live sameaccountwrites/transfer/failures | 默认unsupported；结果记录，无matrix/ADR不扩展stable承诺 |

Drop-in artifact replacement、data compatibility、quiesced rolling、zero-downtime/live writes分開報告（定義04§15）；零停機不是既有承諾。ProposedCapability不证明未知peer真实行为，oldpeer永远按legacy处理。

## Report模板／release gates

| 字段 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| Status | PASS / FAIL / BLOCKED / NOT_RUN（不混用） |
| Artifacts | actualSHA/version/buildsettings |
| Runtime | exactserver/JDK/optionaldependency/storageversions |
| Case | test/scenario/profile、input、faultpoint |
| Expected/Observed | 各自独立，附assertion/record |
| Triage / priority | 08 finding ID、CONFIRMED/DISPROVED/INCONCLUSIVE/DEFERRED、issue priority與review；不與測試PASS/FAIL混用 |
| Remainingrisk | coldVaulttradeoff、hardcrashwindow、mixedwriters、CMIclock等 |
| Evidence | ignoredreport路径与redactedsummary；不提交secrets/logs |

Alpha/Beta/Stable规则依04§24。没有realFolia/CMI/network证据不可用unit/build作为替代；明确blocking或缩窄支持声明需maintainerreview。

## Acceptance routing

V03正式support與歷史研究分開。所有CONFIRMED [P0]需修復／regression關閉；[P0-CANDIDATE]按08四種triage及review處理，未再現不自動成blocker；ADR-03/04/06/07在相關product/implementation/freeze前核准。Read-only SDK absence/shutdown/error contract、兩份descriptor api-version1.21.6、Paper1.21.6實際載入及最新supported Paper state upgrade不可被performance例外豁免。
public mutation/idempotency、Aeron transport與Agrona primitives、full engine redesign不是本matrix的隱含前置。Report增加priority與ADR approval／performance exception欄，NOT_RUN不算PASS。

The first Paper 1.21.6 smoke used the local PlugDev-prepared profile. For the final candidate rerun, the same ignored profile, Paper JAR, plugin dependencies, and test data were launched through Java 25 console stdin because the CLI did not retain a session in this headless worktree process. The runtime result is directly recorded by the acceptance probe and server log.

Pre-change environment observation: `java -version` reports Oracle JDK 25.0.4; `gradlew.bat --version` on the existing wrapper reports Gradle 9.1.0 with launcher 21.0.6 and daemon JDK 21 (`JAVA_HOME`). The T00 Java 21.0.6 / Gradle 9.1.0 baseline is recorded above and its raw logs are preserved under ignored `build/reports/foundation/`.

CodeQL review dispositions (independent review completed 2026-10-05):

The first Java/Kotlin CodeQL database capture did not capture source through the Gradle daemon. The rerun used Gradle `--no-daemon --rerun-tasks`, then completed source capture and analysis successfully (243/243 Java files). The JavaScript/TypeScript scan used a clean mirror of tracked plus non-ignored current source and captured 91/91 JS/TS files and 3/3 GitHub Actions files. Final SARIF invocation status is successful for both runs. The first failed capture is not counted as a passed scan; no findings were suppressed.

- `java/toctou-race-condition`, `PlayerTransactionGuard.java:142`, severity 7.7; SARIF related locations are :118 and :137. The message links state use at :142 to a previous call at :137. Source shows separate synchronized `incrementTransactionsPerSecond()` and `incrementTransactionsPerMinute(amount)` methods updating distinct counters; no invariant requiring atomicity across the pair was identified. Independent review disposition: **likely false positive for an unsafe shared-state race**, based on source analysis. Runtime quota stress was NOT RUN, so no general rate-limit coverage claim is made.
- Separate independent-review observation: `TransferOrchestrator` calls receiver `deposit` before `checkAndLockReceiverForTransfer`. **INCONCLUSIVE; not reproduced; not a CodeQL alert.** A withdrawal may race after the deposit returns and before the receiver lock is applied; no funds-invariant violation is established. T01 owns a barrier-controlled interleaving after receiver deposit commit and before lock apply; record caller/mode/observed result and assess the documented guard contract.
- Six `js/empty-password-in-configuration-file` alerts at `src/main/resources/config.yml:33, 44, 398, 461, 470, 485`: **conditional operator-configuration risks.** They map to optional Redis, database, migration-CMI, and Shadow Sync SQL password fields. Tracked defaults use localhost for Redis/database/migration CMI; Shadow Sync selects SQLite, leaving its SQL variants dormant. The plugin passes configured values through and does not require a nonempty password. Deployments selecting remote services must supply credentials and protect endpoints. Credential values were not inspected or printed. Independent review accepted this bounded disposition; it is not a claim that remote deployments are secure by default.
- Two `js/missing-origin-check` alerts at line 23 in the generated and source MSW worker assets: **production false positives under the current boundary.** `syncmoney-web/src/main.ts` starts MSW only when `import.meta.env.DEV` is true. Development-server exposure remains the relevant condition.
- Independent T00A review found no remaining CodeQL gate blocker. It also found a P2 preview-label mismatch: CI uses repository variable `NEXT_VERSION`, currently documented as `1.3.3`, while the local candidate build used `1.4.0-alpha.1`. This does not change T00A's local artifact or stable metadata; defer target preview-line selection to T22 before preview release work.

Previously open: wrapper/checksum, Java 25 build/tests/coverage, classfile 69/consumer, dependency compatibility, minimum runtime smoke, CodeQL execution and independent review are now complete. CodeQL has reviewed findings; exit 0 does not mean zero findings. Latest Paper/Folia/Canvas and full state-upgrade coverage remain assigned to T21/V03.

## Executed evidence — T01 characterization (2026-10-05)

Source under test: commit `716f219600fe87a0d63e8451c3c6f8551370fd2a`; fixtures are local working-tree tests. JDK: Oracle JDK 25.0.4 (`C:\Program Files\Java\jdk-25.0.4`), Gradle wrapper 9.8.0. No production source changed.

| Case / command | Observed result | Triage note |
|---|---|---|
| `.\gradlew.bat test --tests '*OutcomeTest' --tests '*CompensationTest' --tests '*FailureSemanticsTest' --tests '*AdmissionFailureTest' --tests '*PendingStateTest' --tests '*TransferOrchestratorGuardWindowTest'` | **14 tests, 10 assertion failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped; exit 1.** Four characterization/control tests passed. Failures below are expected safety assertions exposing current behavior, not a passing regression suite. Fresh verification rerun on 2026-10-05: same 14/10/0/0 outcome, exit 1. Raw output: ignored `build/reports/foundation/T01-aggregate-review.log`. | T01 aggregate executed against the final reviewed fixture shape; failing cases remain release/decision inputs. |
| E01 concurrent rejected withdrawal | A and B start at `100.00/v7`. B withdraws `100.00`; A resumes after insufficient funds. A returns `0.00` instead of `-1`; two withdrawals are queued and two success posts observed. Final memory is still `0.00/v8`. | CONFIRMED incorrect-success for the direct non-LOCAL queue-backed `TransactionWriter.withdraw` path with `EventSource.TEST`; no memory-funds corruption is claimed. |
| E02 receiver pre-event cancellation | Transfer returns failure. After draining the accepted sender event through the real consumer into a fake balance adapter, reload yields sender `90.00`, receiver `0`, total `90.00` from `100.00`. | Confirms the unpaired sender withdrawal in this writer/consumer path. The balance adapter is a deterministic fake; no live Redis/SQL durability claim. |
| E02 throwing post observer | A `RuntimeException` from a receiver-deposit `PostTransactionEvent` observer is caught by `SyncmoneyEventBus`; transfer remains accepted with both legs queued and net delta zero. | The specific runtime-observer-throw compensation candidate is DISPROVED for the internal event bus path. Does not cover `Error` or failures outside listener dispatch. |
| E03 full queue + unavailable overflow | Queue is prefilled; configured `RedisOverflowLog` sees Redis unavailable. `TransactionWriter.deposit` returns `110.00`, changes memory `100.00/v7` to `110.00/v8`, and emits success post; only the earlier queue entry remains. Source inspection shows `RedisOverflowLog` falls back to `System.err`, not a recoverable file record. | CONFIRMED acceptance without queue admission for this configured Redis-overflow failure path. The fixture does not test an unwritable file-based OverflowLog or process-restart loss. |
| E04 Redis fallback | Withdrawal `delta=-10.00`, current `100.00`: fallback sets `110.00`; SET target `40.00`, current `80.00`: fallback sets `20.00`. | CONFIRMED wrong signed-delta and absolute-SET behavior in the exercised consumer branches. |
| E05 Redis success then DB enqueue failure | Fake Redis adapter applies `+10.00` twice (`100.00→110.00→120.00`); fake `DbWriteQueue.offer` is attempted twice after its first injected exception. | CONFIRMED duplicate ADD after a downstream exception in this consumer path. This is not evidence of ordinary queue saturation (offer returning false) or a live SQL outage; T06/T08 own backend failure and recovery validation. |
| E06 version/pending ownership | Equal-version conflicting remote update returns `true` without changing the resident `100.00/v7`. After accepted local `110.00/v8` is expired, old remote `100.00/v7` returns `true` and replaces memory with `100.00/v7`; the deposit remains queued. Newer v8 is applied; older v6 is rejected. A read-only expired entry is eligible for eviction. | Confirms a stale in-memory projection can replace an accepted pending projection after eviction; fixture does not establish durable funds loss. Read-only cache eviction alone did not show a money violation. |
| E07 plugin wrapper gates | Successful `pluginDeposit` and `pluginWithdraw` each emit two pre-events, perform two breaker checks and two completions. Each operation emits one success post, but its `source` is the generic `PLUGIN_*` value and neither `source` nor `reason` retains `ExampleShop`. A separately cancelled plugin deposit leaves balance/version, queue, and post events unchanged. | Duplicate gates and missing per-operation caller attribution are CONFIRMED for the tested wrappers; cancellation control passed. The fixture does not treat `errorMessage` as attribution. |
| E08 shutdown with failed downstream retry | The first fake DB enqueue blocks after Redis applies +10.00; stop() is requested, then the enqueue throws. The revised safety fixture calls shutdown() before checking for retry. It observes no second DB offer; its Redis counter remains one. | CONFIRMED no downstream retry was observed in this shutdown path. This uses an injected DbWriteQueue.offer exception, not a live SQL outage; no durable recovery artifact or process-restart behavior was exercised. T08/T15 own the recovery contract. |
| Cold Vault; `.\gradlew.bat :test --tests 'noietime.syncmoney.vault.VaultTest' --rerun-tasks` | **9 tests, 0 failures/errors/skips; exit 0.** Available cached `735.25` is returned without warming memory. When the cache adapter returns zero despite a seeded SQL `735.25`, cold Vault returns zero and does not query SQL; a later deposit loads SQL and projects `745.25`. The adapter call occurs on the Vault caller thread. Fresh verification rerun on 2026-10-05: 9/0/0/0, exit 0. Raw output: ignored `build/reports/foundation/T01-vault-cold-review.log`. | The zero-on-cache-result behavior is reproduced with fakes; the later SQL load is counterevidence to overwrite in this fixture. It does not prove actual blocking I/O, live outage handling, or durable behavior. Real Redis/SQL and timing remain untested; ADR-04 was open at this historical checkpoint and is resolved under T04 below. |
| Transfer receiver-lock window; also included in aggregate | With real `PlayerTransactionGuard` enabled and receiver threshold `5`, the barrier pauses before lock application. A concurrent receiver withdrawal of `5` succeeds; after the barrier, the transfer installs its lock and a further withdrawal is rejected. Queue deltas sum to the explicit `-5` withdrawal. | Ordering window CONFIRMED; no funds creation/destruction beyond that explicit withdrawal. Not a P0 funds finding; policy/failure semantics remain for maintainer review. |

The first implementation of several fixtures was rejected by independent review because it required the current broken path (for example, waiting for two Redis mutations before asserting there should be one, requiring pending eviction, or using a throwing overflow mock). Those assertions were corrected: the downstream retry fixture checks for a second DB offer after shutdown() without accepting a second Redis mutation; transfer tests permit zero-leg rejection or safe compensation; pending tests do not require eviction; queue saturation uses the configured RedisOverflowLog; plugin attribution is checked independently for both operations and excludes errorMessage. Final fixture re-review is recorded in 05/08. The independent full-triage review supports the scoped E01–E06 CONFIRMED/P0, E07 CONFIRMED/P1, and E08 INCONCLUSIVE/P0-CANDIDATE dispositions; it does not upgrade fake-backend observations into live SQL/Redis or restart evidence. The stale ADR-07 summary was corrected. T01 review is complete; ADR-07 maintainer approval remains a separate gate.

NOT RUN for T01: live Redis/Lua failure and wrong-key cases (T06), MySQL/PostgreSQL delayed stale writer (T05), Paper/Folia/Canvas runtime, CMI clock/freshness (T13), real LOCAL SQLite blocking/overwrite (T04), unwritable file-based OverflowLog, and process-restart durability. E05/E08 fake DbWriteQueue.offer exceptions are not described as ordinary queue saturation or SQL outages. These are not inferred PASS; 08 records each owner and next evidence.

Decision recorded from the user on 2026-10-05: when `MigrationLock` is active, reject all external writes, including `ADMIN_SET` and `COMMAND_ADMIN`; permit exactly the migration worker's own one-shot `ADMIN_SET`. The permit is consumed at `TransactionWriter.setBalance` entry so synchronous callbacks cannot reuse it. `MigrationTask` now treats null/negative rejected results as player migration failure before backup checkpoint, SQL, or optional Redis effects.

CMI source quiescence is resolved under ADR-11. Before snapshot, Syncmoney requires configured CMI economy command/module auto-disable, writes those settings, and requires Bukkit to accept the reload command. This is not runtime proof that arbitrary third-party CMI API or SQL writers are stopped; operators must quiesce all external CMI source writers during copy. A detected non-echo balance event invalidates the snapshot, blocks further migration permits, fails the checkpoint, and prevents completion. The partial target must not be activated and must be replaced by a fresh `-force` migration after source quiescence. CMI emits the event after authority mutation, so Syncmoney cannot veto arbitrary direct API calls. Runtime reload semantics and actual CMI migration remain NOT RUN. Unidentifiable non-echo balance events conservatively dirty the snapshot; outbound-suppressed echoes do not.

The E06 review also reproduced a synthetic completion receipt pairing an earlier write balance with a later observed cache version. `MemoryPendingStateTest.deferredRemoteBalanceWinsWhenCompletionReceiptCarriesItsVersion` failed before the fix and passed after `MemoryStateManager` began preferring a deferred remote snapshot on an equal version. Independent review confirms that this closes the observed E06 memory reconciliation race without adding a supported third-party API or storage contract. Exact balance/version receipt pairing for subsequent publish and SQL work remains with T05 and is not claimed fixed by T03.

Terminal retry ownership discovery: after maximum retries, `processFailedEvents` removes the event from its retry map without completing its reservation. The reservation and deferred account state remain retained, consuming one bounded global admission slot. This is fail-closed and can reduce admission capacity until restart/recovery or manual reconciliation. `OverflowLogInterface.log` does not report durable acknowledgment, and `RedisOverflowLog` may fall back to stderr; releasing accepted ownership without stronger evidence could lose the event. The current T03 disposition is to preserve ownership; no recovery/acknowledgment format or behavior was added.

NOT RUN: real Redis/SQL, actual CMI migration/runtime reload, LOCAL SQLite migration, restart/crash recovery, Folia/Canvas, and PlugDev E03/E06 scenarios. `:syncmoney-core:test` was not run because that module/scaffold does not exist; T03 used the existing root test source set to avoid expanding the bounded repair into a module split. T03 is COMPLETE for approved E03/E06 scope and the ADR-11 operational contract; no arbitrary external writer fence or live CMI result is claimed. T04 is COMPLETE and T05 is next under the dependency graph. The full root suite remains owner-routed, not a PASS.

### T03 source-content fingerprint follow-up — 2026-10-07

The first independent recovery review reproduced an unsafe resume: source identity and count stayed fixed while a row balance changed, allowing old checkpoint offset data to be combined with a changed source. T03 was reopened to bind resume to migration-relevant content in the existing checkpoint. No new recovery format, engine, or public contract was added.

Fingerprint fields are length-prefixed UTF-8, with an explicit null-UUID marker and a versioned format marker; balances use the existing two-decimal HALF_UP normalization. The digest omits fields that do not affect migration output. For single-source SQLite/MySQL readers, fingerprint and page queries share the same ordering keys. Multi-source candidates are canonicalized before merge because strategy ties otherwise depended on unspecified database row order.

NOT RUN for this follow-up: real/licensed CMI source runtime and migration, cross-database snapshot isolation, arbitrary third-party CMI API/SQL writer behavior during or after the fingerprint scan, live Redis/SQL, process crash/power-loss, Folia/Canvas, and PlugDev migration acceptance. The SHA fingerprint protects paused/resumed content only under ADR-11's operator quiescence precondition; it is not a live fence for external writers.

## Executed evidence — T04 (COMPLETE under ADR-04 and §21, 2026-10-06)

Base: T03 commit `b8867c8`; T04 implementation commit `2247cb8`; test-only shutdown characterization commit `bef602359fd3bf64a2f65819c86cca3f1b109785`; shared queue shutdown repair commit `8326daa` on branch `linco/syncmoney-1.4.0-architecture-foundation-t00`. Java/toolchain: Oracle JDK 25.0.4, Gradle 9.8.0. The minimum runtime is Paper 1.21.6 per ADR-01. T04 added bounded async Placeholder loading and a LOCAL SQLite persistence worker using the existing queue/transaction tables; it adds an internal checksummed LOCAL journal and additive request-id dedup under the user's delegated technical authority. No stable SDK contract, Redis wire format, or shared SQL format was added.

Environment discovery for the final rerun: PowerShell's `java` resolved to Oracle JDK 25.0.4 while `JAVA_HOME` still pointed to JDK 21 and the default Gradle launcher/daemon were 21.0.6. The final focused T04 and full-root reruns explicitly set `JAVA_HOME` and `PATH` to Oracle JDK 25.0.4; `gradlew --version` confirmed both launcher and daemon were 25.0.4. Decision gates: ADR-04 and §21 are resolved for T04. Cold Vault/VaultUnlocked behavior returns unavailable (`NaN`/`null`) while the bounded loader warms the account; cold mutations are rejected. Graceful LOCAL shutdown retains accepted ownership through receipt or the checksummed journal handoff, and startup replays before LOCAL readiness. The configured 10-second deadline, durable journal handoff, SQLite request-id dedup, and fail-closed retention when journal persistence fails are covered by focused tests. Actual CMI migration/runtime reload, external Vault consumers, live shared Redis/SQL, permanent backend outage, forced process kill/power-loss, Folia, and Canvas remain NOT RUN. A hard crash before journal handoff can still lose an accepted in-memory LOCAL write; no hard-crash durability claim is made. T04 and T05 are COMPLETE; T06 is next under the dependency graph. The latest full root suite has one E02/T07 failure; E05/E08 owner work remains with T06/T08 and outside T04.

PlugDev rerun note: the first detached launch stopped before acceptance because the existing `.plugdev/run/.plugdev-alive` heartbeat was stale; that attempt yielded no test result. After confirming PlugDev reported the server stopped and refreshing only that generated heartbeat, the warm acceptance and same-profile restart rows above were run on the corrected alpha candidate. No PlugDev source or public npm package was modified or used.

Implementation commit: 3b85b61. T06 resolved its E05 same-process retry path without adding a Redis operation marker, new recovery format, or public contract. On REJECTED or SENT_UNKNOWN, accepted consumer work is quarantined in memory and is not automatically replayed; an unknown response remains ambiguous absent a confirmed receipt. /pay keeps per-sender in-process protection. This does not establish exactly-once execution or quarantine across process restart. T08 owns durable recovery; ordinary queue saturation/offer(false), live deployed SQL/Redis failure, Paper/Folia/Canvas runtime, hard crash/power loss, and mixed-version behavior remain NOT RUN. ### T08 start / ADR-15 decision — 2026-10-08

- T07 is COMPLETE; T08 is IN PROGRESS on `linco/syncmoney-1.4.0-architecture-foundation-t00` at `76fc4c37d37bdf984118ee04373a76128c8520c8` before implementation.
- Source evidence: `OverflowLog.readAndClear()` clears the file after reading; `RedisOverflowLog.readAndClear()` deletes the Redis ZSET and silently drops JSON `balanceAfter`/`version` when converting to the legacy six-field format. Redis overflow also deletes old records after 24 hours or over 100,000 entries; fallback writes only to stderr. `EconomyEventConsumer` can retain a confirmed receipt in its in-memory retry map, but its shutdown drain currently does not durably checkpoint that state.
- ADR-15 ruling: add a separate internal checksummed `recovery-v1.jsonl` journal. Persist exact receipts for confirmed downstream retries, preserve NOT_SENT/REJECTED/ATTEMPTING distinctions, and never auto-replay an ambiguous legacy record. Keep `overflow.log` and the Redis legacy ZSET untouched during read; pending/ambiguous new-format records block rollback to 1.3.2. No public API, SQL schema, Redis protocol, or hard-crash/power-loss guarantee is added.
- Required tests/gates are still pending. T08 implementation must prove append/ack restart behavior, partial/corrupt preservation, disk/compaction failure retention, legacy SET ambiguity, queue-empty retry drain, and confirmed retry without a second Redis mutation. Raw test logs will be recorded here after execution.

Config merge keeps operator values/custom keys and only advances config schema after adding missing defaults; backup and temporary replacement failures are fail-safe. Discovery/ruling: an early version normalized `AccountState.balance` in its constructor and caused scale-sensitive root assertions to fail (the raw failure output was not retained). Existing `EconomyState` is a passive record, so constructor normalization changes `BigDecimal.equals` semantics. Preserve the supplied representation in core snapshots and leave HALF_UP normalization at existing mutation boundaries; the focused `AccountStateTest` now fixes this compatibility behavior, while `MoneyPolicyTest` verifies `1.005 -> 1.01`.

NOT RUN: PlugDev/Paper runtime acceptance for this extraction, Folia, Canvas, external Vault consumer matrix, and live shared Redis/SQL. The full Gradle gate and packaging do not establish those runtime claims. T12 is COMPLETE for its pure-core boundary; T13 is next.

Covers MGET/MSET call shape, incomplete pair rejection, clock rollback/skew/counter wrap/restart equality/long precision, owner callback freshness, retirement/offline, echo suppression, and watermark TTL expiry. Mockito verifies Redis command shape; it is not live Redis evidence. |
| JDK 25.0.4: `:test --rerun-tasks --no-daemon --console=plain` | **PASS, exit 0:** root 466/466, 0 failures/errors/skips; `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` in 1m 10s. | Fresh root regression run after pair repair. Gradle emitted existing Java instrumentation/native-access and deprecation warnings; no test was skipped. |
| `shadowJar --no-daemon --console=plain` on JDK 25.0.4 | **PASS, exit 0.** | Confirms production plugin artifact assembly with the new internal helper. No release or publication was performed. |
| JDK 25.0.4 `javap -public -s` on `CMIVersioning` and `CMIPubsubHandler` | **PASS:** `generateVersion(AtomicLong)` descriptor remains `(Ljava/util/concurrent/atomic/AtomicLong;)J`; original five-argument public `CMIPubsubHandler` constructor descriptor remains unchanged. | Package-private Clock seams do not replace existing public descriptors. Numeric wire field remains a `long`. |
| Independent post-repair source review | **No actionable finding.** | Reviewer confirmed both pair writes use MSET, join read uses MGET, and unavailable/invalid snapshots skip reconciliation. No tests or live runtime were run by reviewer. |
| Licensed CMI artifact/runtime inventory in Syncmoney worktree and local PlugDev project | **BLOCKED / NOT FOUND:** no `*CMI*.jar` available. | V05 explicitly requires a maintainer-provided licensed CMI JAR and prohibits downloading an unauthorized JAR. Mocks cannot satisfy this gate. Actual CMI event/reflection/entity behavior remains unverified. |
| `git diff --check` | **PASS** after final tracked-source update. | Whitespace validation only; line-ending conversion advisories are not whitespace errors. |

Ruling under ADR-16: the P1 mirror-pair requirement is satisfied with MGET/MSET over the existing keys; no CAS/SET-Lua or new ordering guarantee is selected. Independent review found a remaining P2 hypothesis: after watermark expiry/eviction, conflicting equal-version callbacks could share the same local watermark and both pass callback equality checks. This case was not directly exercised and no live CMI evidence establishes a money defect; it remains a documented P2 follow-up, not a P0 finding. `expiredWatermarkAllowsAnOlderInboundVersionAgain` does characterize that an older version can be accepted after TTL expiry.

T13 local implementation and E1 gates are complete; **overall T13 status remains BLOCKED on V05 licensed CMI acceptance**. Sequencing ruling (2026-10-09): T14's explicit dependency is T13's necessary CMI contract and assembly prerequisites, satisfied by ADR-16, local pair/clock/freshness evidence, and preserved public descriptors. T14 may proceed with source assembly and packaging checks; this does not waive V05 or establish runtime behavior. Real CMI acceptance remains NOT RUN/BLOCKED and is required before T21 can claim CMI runtime coverage or the release can pass. E07 remains P1. E08 remains INCONCLUSIVE / P0-CANDIDATE and outside mandatory correctness scope. Live Redis, real CMI, multi-server CMI ordering, Folia, and Canvas remain NOT RUN. T13 code/tests are committed as `6aa85fc`; aligned documentation is committed as `64a2b42`.

Confirmed behavior: blank, change-me, and change-me-in-production keys (including case/whitespace variants) prevent WebAdminServer startup; Syncmoney initialization continues after WebServiceManager returns. Every routed /ws request including upgrade and OPTIONS returns 503 without a 101-shaped response. SSE tokens are one-time, atomically consumed, and expire at 60 seconds; missing/invalid SSE authentication returns 401. Ordinary API CORS preflight remains behind the /ws-specific route check and generic preflight handler.

The precondition runs before backend connection/namespace creation. The method-scoped SQLite rerun above passed. This failed selector is not counted as SQL backend evidence. |

Reviewers ran no tests except the boundary reviewer’s `:syncmoney-protocol:test --rerun-tasks --no-daemon --console=plain`, which passed. |

Confirmed behavior: the version-aware fakes reject stale snapshots and conflicting equal-version snapshots, distinguish pre-commit rejection from post-commit unknown outcome, and deliver duplicates/out-of-order callbacks only through a bounded manual executor. Shared 1.3.2 protocol and local SQLite fixtures contain synthetic IDs/schema and no copied operator configuration, credentials, or player data. The disposable SQL namespace helper creates only a random schema/database name and drops that same owned name; SQLite uses a private temporary file. Integration tests use observed lock state plus a bounded deadline rather than sleeping to guess that a race occurred.

NOT RUN: live PostgreSQL, MySQL, and MariaDB helper/backend behavior. All three `SYNCMONEY_TEST_*_URL` variables were absent; no live SQL endpoint was contacted or changed. The SQL helper source compiles and is wired into the real-backend integration test, but no live backend claim is made. Seven old `%TEMP%\syncmoney-redis-test-*` directories from earlier failed launches remain outside the repository; each has only `redis.log`, no PID file, no process whose command line references it, and no listener on its logged port. A shell safety policy rejected the requested cleanup command, so the inactive logs were preserved. The passing Redis integration run created no additional residual directory.

Confirmed behavior: every Portal upload request is pinned to `https://central.sonatype.com/api/v1/publisher`, uses `publishingType=USER_MANAGED`, and refuses redirects. The input is copied once to a bounded private snapshot; signature verification, GAV/content hashing, ZIP inspection, and the upload all use those exact bytes. Upload intent and GAV/content hashes are written before the POST; an ambiguous upload result becomes `UPLOAD_UNKNOWN` and cannot be blindly retried. Invalid status shapes/types and status timeouts fail closed as `STATUS_UNKNOWN`; a later invocation resumes status polling without another upload. A changed content hash for the same GAV and a recorded `FAILED` deployment both stop before upload. `VALIDATED` does not imply public Maven Central availability and this client never publishes it.

NOT RUN: actual Central upload or authenticated staging resolution; namespace ownership proof; account-specific publisher tier/usage classification or eligibility; production signing credentials; Folia/Paper/Canvas runtime (not applicable to this publishing task); GitHub Actions execution. Account-level publication checks and any authorized public publish remain T23 gates. Next task by plan order: T19; its performance branch remains independent of T18.

Result list has exactly 192 rows and 192 distinct expected `(variant, operation, accessPattern, accountCount, mode, threads)` keys: two variants × eight operations × two access patterns × three account counts × two JMH modes, one producer. Every row has 3 forks, Java 25.0.4, JMH 1.37, finite score, and `-XX:+UseSerialGC -Dnet.bytebuddy.experimental=true -Xmx1g`. The manifest runId/result path/source SHA/baseline SHA match the run; benchmark JAR SHA-256 matches the manifest; manifest GC flags match every row. There are 96 SampleTime rows and 96 manifest tail assessments. For every sample row, manifest sampleCount equals recomputed `rawDataHistogram` counts; all 96 meet the configured 100,000 minimum and are marked p999Reliable. Audit output: `ROWS=192 UNIQUE=192/192 SAMPLE_ROWS=96 TAIL_COUNT=96 ALL_P999_RELIABLE=True`, `VALIDATION_ERRORS=0`.
- **Correctness evidence:** `TransactionBenchmarks.verifyAndClose` (`@TearDown(Level.Trial)`) checks await-drain, adapter failure/zero queue, drained accepted write legs, offered=accepted+rejected, every seeded account's balance/version/receipt, SET target receipt, transfer/write funds conservation, nonnegative balances, rejected control immutability, and cancellation no-queue/no-receipt invariants. A JMH worker teardown error invalidates the task; this execution completed every 192-row result with no ERROR/exception/failure marker and Gradle exit 0. Thus all configured 3-fork trial gates completed without a thrown correctness failure. Receipts are deterministic in-memory adapter completions only; no SQL/Redis durability claim.
- **Performance review gate failed; no values were hidden or filtered.** Independently joined the current and legacy132 rows by operation/accessPattern/accountCount/mode and compared current vs 1.3.2. Throughput `>=90%`: 11/48 pass, 37/48 fail (median ratio 68.85%, range 0.85%–158.54%). `gc.alloc.rate.norm` allocation/op `<=110%`: 6/48 pass, 42/48 fail (median ratio 188.12%; no baseline-zero cases). SampleTime p99 `<=110%`: 15/48 pass, 33/48 fail (median ratio 159.86%, range 53.97%–10,240%; all sample p99.9 counts meet the minimum). These are synthetic synchronous façade-acceptance measurements, not SQL/Redis latency. Several legacy rejection p99s are only 0.2–0.3 us and should be treated as timer-floor-sensitive despite sufficient histogram counts. **Decision:** keep the regression visible; do not claim a performance pass or grant a performance exception yet. Required cost attribution/profile review and the remaining planned runs are still open. Correctness results do not erase the regression, and the regression does not waive money-safety gates.
- **r7 operation-group diagnostic slice (not causal attribution):** independently recomputed medians over the six matched `(accessPattern, accountCount)` pairs per operation. Ratios are current / legacy132; p99 values are microseconds. This makes the regression distribution visible and guides profiling; it does not replace the 48-case gate or establish why costs differ.

  | operation | throughput ratio | allocation/op ratio | current / legacy p99 (µs) |
  |---|---:|---:|---:|
  | balance | 123.98% | 0.01% | 0.10 / 0.10 |

  The rejection p99 comparison is especially timer-floor-sensitive: the baseline median is 0.20 µs. The current normal write path also takes `MigrationLock.tryAcquireWrite()` and closes a `WriteLease`, which enters the shared monitor; this is a **profiling hypothesis**, not a demonstrated cause. Other attribution candidates are transaction prechecks and the benchmark-only asynchronous receipt consumer. The receipt consumer is synthetic in-memory work and has different current/legacy internals; it is intentionally not described as SQL/Redis latency or production persistence cost. Profile matched current/baseline write, rejection, and cancellation paths before deciding on optimization or any delegated performance exception.

- **T19 read-only drain-path trace — 2026-10-10 18:31:21 +08:00 (E0 source evidence; paired t=2 r1 still active).** EconomyEventConsumer polls accepted events from EconomyWriteQueue before processing; CurrentFacadeBenchmarkAdapter does the same. This source-level behavior is a plausible mechanism for the four observed backlog drain timeouts, but does not alone prove the complete timeout cause or quantify production impact. - **T19 repair hypothesis recorded before code changes.** Add a bounded identity-based membership tracker for events that are still physically queued. Commit marks before `offer`; successful `poll` clears membership before returning; queued cancellation/completion removes from the queue and clears membership. Already-dequeued completion must skip `removeIf`; queued cancellation must still remove the envelope and release its pending count. No public contract or queue engine change. First regression uses a real instrumented `LinkedBlockingQueue` to count visited backlog entries and is expected to fail on the current code.


### T19 queue membership and interruptible poll repair — 2026-10-10

- **Review finding and disposition.** A read-only concurrency review identified a window where LinkedBlockingQueue.poll had physically removed an event but queuedEvents still marked it queued. A concurrent complete/quarantine could therefore scan the remaining backlog. The barrier regression failed before linearization: it observed 2 backlog predicate inspections (expected 0), and completion did not wait for the dequeue transition. Fixed.
- **Interruption finding and disposition.** The first monitor-based implementation failed the pre-interrupted-poll regression because it could dequeue an available event without throwing InterruptedException. Replaced that draft with ReentrantLock.lockInterruptibly and a Condition. Poll checks interruption before the immediate dequeue, waits with awaitNanos, and returns the remaining timeout; signal/enqueue and dequeue/membership/removal transitions use the same lock. Poll releases this lock before taking commitGate.
- **Focused verification.** RED command for the race: .\gradlew.bat :syncmoney-bukkit:test --tests '*AdmissionReservationTest.completionCannotObserveHalfFinishedDequeueTransition' --rerun-tasks --no-daemon --console=plain; it failed with 2 inspected backlog events. RED command for the monitor draft: .\gradlew.bat :syncmoney-bukkit:test --tests '*AdmissionReservationTest.interruptedPollDoesNotDequeueAnAvailableEnvelope' --rerun-tasks --no-daemon --console=plain; the test failed. After the final lock/condition repair, the four focused cases (dequeue/completion barrier, pre-interrupted poll, queued identity completion, dequeued completion no-scan) passed with Gradle exit 0. The full Bukkit test suite subsequently passed.
- **Full local gate.** Command: .\gradlew.bat test :syncmoney-bukkit:test :syncmoney-papi-expansion:test shadowJar :syncmoney-papi-expansion:jar acceptanceJar :syncmoney-benchmarks:test :syncmoney-benchmarks:benchmarkJar :syncmoney-benchmarks:verifyBenchmarkIsolation :syncmoney-benchmarks:loadTestClasses :syncmoney-benchmarks:verifyBenchmarkProvenance verifyArchitecture verifyPackaging --rerun-tasks --no-daemon --console=plain. Result: BUILD SUCCESSFUL, exit 0, 59 actionable Gradle tasks; test XML totals 651 tests across 137 suites, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped. Architecture, packaging, benchmark isolation and provenance passed. Source-tree SHA-256 6d5398ab9136500d9fb318e898595557211c55bd49c0a5385d06954be74bf9f7; load-test runtime-classpath SHA-256 77659e061c48e4f0ba61840d890d1d75322b8a316831d760c35bc9ef415ae76f (88 entries). Raw gate log: build/reports/foundation/T19-queue-repair-final-gates-r2.log; SHA-256 E9DF45A27084913326349854D73F5493E87F808A107D04156E388F23BA591681. git diff --check passed.
- **Independent review and limits.** The initial IMPORTANT race finding was fixed; a separate re-review found no remaining actionable membership, lock-order, interruption, or accounting issue. The membership review recommendation to count actual predicate visits was adopted. The queue remains bounded and backed by LinkedBlockingQueue; no public API, schema, configuration, Redis wire, or recovery format changed. Prior paired t=2 r1/r2 artifacts remain STOPPED / INVALID. No post-repair paired t=2 or t=8 matrix has run; T19 remains IN_PROGRESS and no performance conclusion is available. Next gate is the full t=2 matrix from the committed source, with all 192 expected rows, report binding, and per-trial postconditions.

### T19 paired t=2 r1 teardown stall and repair — 2026-10-11

- Run disposition: paired t=2 run 2863980a-3608-484a-97cc-f70e059ef080 used candidate 272423a7bf6b9f52b291f2be14a56ed45c070f53, baseline 71cc55dfeefe9a8f40a3835b051ddd324d353767, Java 25.0.4, JMH 1.37, SerialGC, 1 GiB, two producers, 5×1s warmup / 10×1s measurement / 3 forks. The Gradle session was interrupted (exit 1) because the full trial could not progress. There was no correctness exception or failed postcondition marker. The JSON result is 0 bytes; no validation summary or manifest exists. The run cannot be accepted and provides no performance comparison. Monitor log SHA-256: C28F31AC889CC08BE32B5369EF5C1FB1E6907459571A5226CFFD73A7A0566207.
- Root cause evidence: both adapters synchronized queue polling and drain snapshots on drainStateLock, but the drainer held that monitor during a 50ms timed queue poll. awaitDrain could therefore block before its nominal deadline check. Legacy fork dump build/reports/foundation/T19-paired-full-t2-postfix-r1-fork-24000-dump.txt (elapsed 123.94s; SHA-256 296948CFE13973132477E9C4756F26FE823C17915E6E6FEF956E6884AD697349) showed LegacyFacadeBenchmarkAdapter.isDrained waiting for the same monitor held by the drainer in LinkedBlockingQueue.poll(50ms). Current adapter dump build/reports/foundation/T19-paired-full-t2-postfix-r1-fork-34564-dump.txt (SHA-256 B8337E9D7798D1EDDDC9A1F37E50A91C79345E1B00D65294A8ABB3F48695628D) showed CurrentFacadeBenchmarkAdapter.drainSnapshot blocked while the drainer held the monitor in EconomyWriteQueue.poll. The later current dump build/reports/foundation/T19-paired-full-t2-postfix-r1-fork-34564-dump-r2.txt at 01:29:44 (elapsed 324.35s; SHA-256 7E80E2D2FFA71A77067E3140FD50F044D3216F7116ADD989DEDE07BA6ACE6FE1) showed JMH main waiting for task completion, one worker sleeping on the generated trial-teardown mutex, and the other worker idle. This is benchmark harness lifecycle contention, not evidence of a production queue correctness defect.
- Regression and fix: CurrentFacadeBenchmarkAdapterDrainTest.emptyQueuePollDoesNotHoldTheDrainSnapshotMonitor failed before the fix; JMX directly observed an empty timed queue poll while holding the snapshot monitor. Both current and legacy adapters now use zero-time polls inside the short synchronized dequeue/inFlight update and sleep for 1ms outside the monitor when empty. The monitor still makes queue removal and inFlight increment atomic; the blocking-wait starvation path is removed.

### T19 paired t=2 performance review and scoped exception — 2026-10-11

- **Bound inputs.** Run `b5f5a11c-8467-4e30-b2d2-80b62d28b59d`; candidate `149e4ad96a42bdd2f7a46dcbb40fb194df6c011b`; baseline `71cc55dfeefe9a8f40a3835b051ddd324d353767`; source-tree SHA-256 `a8e1384928b12e6c62b7e38f07cc444aadd92873ee4fa0a90be897454f773848`. The 192-row JSON is 41,792,929 bytes, SHA-256 `8742140c6fb1b798c6f5f550a03829aed582c8475bdbe55c8eb856e8f4a99b76`, bound by `syncmoney-benchmarks/build/reports/benchmarks/transaction-paired-manifest-t2-b5f5a11c-8467-4e30-b2d2-80b62d28b59d.json`. JDK 25.0.4, JMH 1.37, SerialGC, 1 GiB, 2 producer threads, 5×1s warmup / 10×1s measurement / 3 forks. Independent review verified all expected rows, provenance, manifests, sample counts, per-trial postconditions and root-gate evidence; it found no Critical or correctness blocker.
- **B00 result.** On 48 matched operation/access-pattern/account-count cases: throughput was below 90% of baseline on 18/48; allocation exceeded 110% on 38/48; SampleTime p99 exceeded 110% on 42/48. Example `hot / 1 account / reject-withdraw`: throughput 0.0711 vs 11.6908 ops/µs (0.61%); allocation 5,025.22 vs 192.01 B/op (26.17×); SampleTime p99 21.18 vs 0.40 µs (52.95×). Worst recorded p99 example is `hot / 1,000 accounts / reject-withdraw`, 24.672 vs 0.400 µs (61.68×). The matrix does not suppress rejected cases; `TransactionBenchmarks` seeds a zero-balance control account and asserts the withdrawal rejects without accepted writes or balance/version/receipt mutation.
- **Source-supported cost analysis (not a per-step causal profile).** Candidate `TransactionWriter.withdrawAdmitted` acquires a migration `WriteLease`, validates readiness/version, executes the pre-transaction checks, obtains `MemoryStateManager.tryReserveWrite`, and then enters the account mutation path where insufficient funds are rechecked. `tryReserveWrite` takes a per-account lease and a bounded `EconomyWriteQueue` admission reservation; an insufficient withdrawal closes that unused reservation. In 1.3.2, the analogous writer checks the balance inside `stateManager.compute` and exits before constructing/enqueueing an `EconomyEvent`; it has no reserved-queue admission path on this rejection. Thus the candidate rejection path performs synchronization/admission/allocation work that does not end in a mutation. These gates protect memory/queue admission consistency, bounded backpressure, per-account mutation ordering and migration snapshot fencing. The measured report does not isolate the contribution of each gate or prove any one commit caused the full delta; the ratios are candidate-versus-1.3.2, not a before/after comparison of the T19 teardown fix.
- **Scope and limits.** This is a synthetic in-memory facade benchmark with a receipt adapter, not SQL/Redis throughput or a live Paper/Folia/Canvas run. JMH ran without captured fork CPU affinity; p99.9 intervals satisfy the configured sample minimum but do not establish cross-fork stationarity. The result is valid for its named profile only and must not be presented as production absolute capacity or as platform compatibility evidence.
- **Harness consumer asymmetry found during independent review.** For accepted events the current adapter calls `EconomyWriteQueue.complete/completePair`, exercising reserved-write completion bookkeeping and the current memory-state completion handler. The 1.3.2 adapter records a receipt and invokes `clearPendingTracking`; it does not execute the candidate-style reservation completion callback. The paired run remains a valid, hash-bound observation of these adapters, but it is not a like-for-like end-to-end consumer-cost comparison and cannot isolate writer-only latency. The `reject-withdraw` cases assert zero accepted events and do not enter either receipt-completion branch; the report still does not isolate their writer path from harness scheduling. Before assigning precise causal cost or changing architecture based on these deltas, add a bounded follow-up that aligns or explicitly separates consumer completion work. Do not discard this accepted matrix or alter its thresholds.
- **ADR-08 disposition under the user's delegated engineering authority.** ACCEPT the t=2 performance-threshold exception only to continue the required matched matrix and evaluate producerThreads=8. Keep the original 90% throughput / 110% allocation / 110% p99 thresholds unchanged; no rows are removed. All correctness, no-loss, bounded-admission, and prohibited-blocking gates remain hard blockers and are not waived. This is not a release performance approval: T19 remains IN_PROGRESS until t=8/report gates are recorded, and T22 must retain an explicit performance disposition. Reassess whether T20 is justified after t=8; do not claim measured PostgreSQL/Redis/real-server capacity from this JMH result.
- **Next.** Run only the full paired producerThreads=8 matrix against the same clean baseline and source commit/profile. Do not overlap another measurement. The full-root gate for this unchanged source tree is already evidenced above. Open-loop and real SQL/Redis macro evidence are separate gates and remain NOT RUN unless their exact required endpoints/configuration become available.

### T19 current-only JMH and post-Actor full gate — 2026-10-11

**Task status:** Current-only component JMH suites and the fresh local full gate are complete. T19 remains IN_PROGRESS pending the isolated Redis/SQL macro pilot and its dependent open-loop matrix.

**Discovery and decision:** The initial concurrent Queue actor reused the same `EconomyEvent` identity across admissions. Because the queue tracks event identity, the fixture could falsely present an already-enqueued event as a duplicate admission. Commit `73ca338` creates a fresh event per admission and adds actor/adapter regressions. The corrected two-thread rerun passed measured trial checks. This is a benchmark-fixture defect; it did not reproduce a production queue defect, so no production queue change was made for this finding.

### T19 SHA-width and final local-gate follow-up — 2026-10-11

**Task status:** T19 remains IN_PROGRESS. This follow-up closes local implementation and review for schema-2 harness-source provenance; the commit-bound real Redis/SQL pilot and its dependent 60-scenario open-loop matrix remain outstanding.
